# Agents · S · part 31

887 entries, step-triage to strategist. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **step-triage**: Триаж задачи перед работой над ней — вердикт о пригодности, вопросы владельцу и способ проверки каждого пункта критерия. Ничего не меняет и не публикует. Оркестратор вызывает шаг, передавая номер задачи и больше ничего. ([k-kuzmin/domovoy](https://github.com/k-kuzmin/domovoy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/step-triage.md))
- **step-ui**: Spawn to run the UI step of a Specs build: generate each &lt;Name&gt;UI.ts module via /specs-build-ui with UIKit primitives; report each module's public API. ([lens-studio-devs/ls-extensions](https://github.com/lens-studio-devs/ls-extensions/blob/HEAD/plugins/ls-clad/agents/step-ui.md) · ★20)
- **step-ui-module**: Author exactly one &lt;Name&gt;UI.ts module. Spawned in parallel (one per module) by ls-clad:step-ui, which has already resolved the shared doctrine, theme, icon paths and the module's required public API. ([lens-studio-devs/ls-extensions](https://github.com/lens-studio-devs/ls-extensions/blob/HEAD/plugins/ls-clad/agents/step-ui-module.md) · ★20)
- **step-verifier**: Independent verifier of an implemented step. MUST BE USED after test-engineer completes a step. Re-runs zero-trust traces on the changed region against the step's spec, the task's ACs, and the research evidence. Confirms the step landed AND that no scope creep / regression / new hazards were introduced. Read-only. Cannot approve its own team's work -- must either CONFIRM or REJECT with evidence. ([bradselph/zero-trust-feature-team](https://github.com/bradselph/zero-trust-feature-team/blob/HEAD/agents/step-verifier.md))
- **step-writer**: Use this agent when writing the steps of a tutorial or walkthrough: one action per step, code that works when pasted, expected output, checkpoints that confirm progress, and troubleshooting notes where learners get stuck. ([Ormus-Solutions/LibreCopy-Claude-Code](https://github.com/Ormus-Solutions/LibreCopy-Claude-Code/blob/HEAD/plugins/tutorial-creation/agents/step-writer.md))
- **step-writer**: Writes a skill's Steps and Done when, or a subagent's file, so that every graded case can be passed by following them and every tool reference resolves. Assign after the cases exist. ([talentsia/talentsia-skills](https://github.com/talentsia/talentsia-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/talentsia-skill-builder/agents/step-writer.md))
- **step0-module-agent**: Step 0 of the SDLC pipeline. Takes a large, unstructured problem statement from a human and decomposes it into independently-runnable Modules, each of which will separately go through the full 14-step chain. Runs once per project (or once per major new initiative). Invoke this agent first, before any other pipeline agent, whenever a new project or a large new body of work is starting. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step0-module-agent.md))
- **step0b-solution-architecture-agent**: Runs once per project, immediately after the Module Agent's modules.md is Approved, and before any module proceeds past Step 5 (Test Scenarios) in its own chain. Decides the system architecture that Module Agent's business decomposition did not: tech stack, deployment topology, how modules actually communicate, and where shared concerns (auth, logging) live. Uses the C4 model (Context + Container diagrams) for what the architecture looks like, and Architecture Decision Records — the same format already used throughout this pipeline — for why. Invoke once /modules/modules.md is Approved. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step0b-solution-architecture-agent.md))
- **step1-business-requirements-agent**: Step 1 of the SDLC pipeline. Runs once per module, after the Module Agent has scaffolded the module folder. Captures the business need as a set of Business Requirements (BRs) — big, complete units of business capability, not fragmented micro-requirements. Invoke this agent when a module's folder exists and is ready for its business requirements to be captured. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step1-business-requirements-agent.md))
- **step1-resume-draft**: Creates an initial tailored resume draft from master resume materials and job description. This is Step 1 of the three-step resume creation process. The agent analyzes job requirements, applies cultural profile preferences, and uses the HAM-Z methodology to create a targeted first draft. Always presents cultural profile selection menu before proceeding. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step1-resume-draft.md) · ★26)
- **step1-resume-revise**: Revises an existing base resume toward a new job description using a change manifest and surgical edits. This is the revise-mode Step 1 of the three-step resume process. The agent performs gap analysis between the base resume and the job description, writes an explicit change manifest in which every change is justified by a named JD requirement, copies the base resume, applies only the manifest changes as targeted edits, and verifies with a diff gate that all unchanged content remains byte-identical to the base. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step1-resume-revise.md) · ★26)
- **step10-test-automation-agent**: Step 10 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every test scenario, one at a time, and turns it into an actual automated test at the layer the scenario was tagged for in Step 5. Invoke once 09-implementation.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step10-test-automation-agent.md))
- **step11-test-execution-agent**: Step 11 of the SDLC pipeline. Runs the automated test suite from Step 10 against the implementation from Step 9, and records real results — pass, fail, and the actual evidence (output/logs), not a summary written from memory. Invoke once 10-test-automation.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step11-test-execution-agent.md))
- **step12-improvement-agent**: Step 12 of the SDLC pipeline. Resolves every Fail routed from Test Execution, fixing exactly the failure and nothing more. Invoke once 11-test-execution.md is Sealed (or has routed blockers) for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step12-improvement-agent.md))
- **step13-monitoring-agent**: Step 13 of the SDLC pipeline. Sets up production monitoring for this module against the performance thresholds defined in Step 8, using control bands (rolling baseline + tiered response) rather than a single static alert. This is also the agent that closes the loop — a breached band writes a new BR-style intent for Step 1, restarting the pipeline for the affected module. Invoke once 12-improvement.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step13-monitoring-agent.md))
- **step14-deploy-docs-agent**: Step 14 of the SDLC pipeline. Maintains this module's release record using the Keep a Changelog format — a running "Unreleased" section that accumulates entries continuously from Implementation and Improvement, only sealed into a dated version number at actual release time. Not a one-shot document written at the very end. Invoke continuously as Implementation/Improvement items complete, and finally at release. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step14-deploy-docs-agent.md))
- **step2-functional-requirements-agent**: Step 2 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every approved Business Requirement in a module, one at a time, and produces the full set of Functional Requirements (FRs) that fulfil it. This is where detailed splitting happens — BRs stay coarse, FRs get granular. Invoke this agent once 01-business-requirements.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step2-functional-requirements-agent.md))
- **step2-provenance-check**: Performs comprehensive provenance analysis on Step 1 resume drafts to identify credibility issues, evidence gaps, and risk factors. Uses the CV Provenance Hardening methodology to create detailed analysis reports with specific recommendations for strengthening resume credibility before final draft creation. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step2-provenance-check.md) · ★26)
- **step3-final-resume**: Creates the final hardened resume by incorporating all recommendations from Step 2 provenance analysis. This agent takes the Step 1 draft and Step 2 analysis to produce a credible, defensible final resume that maintains competitive positioning while addressing all credibility concerns and evidence gaps. ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step3-final-resume.md) · ★26)
- **step3-ux-agent**: Step 3 of the SDLC pipeline. First builds the module's full screen inventory — every screen the FRs imply, PLUS the prerequisite/scaffolding screens (signup, login, onboarding, splash, forgot-password, etc.) that business-level requirements routinely never spell out — and prioritizes every screen by feature importance. Then loops over every screen, one at a time, producing flows, structure, states, low-fidelity wireframes (element/button-level), a motion/transition model, and accessibility notes — grounded in deep, per-item research (the full BR/FR corpus, every project source document, live… ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step3-ux-agent.md))
- **step4-cover-letter**: Creates a compelling cover letter with requirements-matching table based on the final Step 3 resume ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step4-cover-letter.md) · ★26)
- **step4-cover-letter-forward**: Creates a forward-facing cover letter proposing a dual-anchored first-90-days plan (evidenced problem + past proof) based on the final Step 3 resume and a skill-level intake interview ([reggiechan74/JobOps](https://github.com/reggiechan74/JobOps/blob/HEAD/plugins/jobops/agents/step4-cover-letter-forward.md) · ★26)
- **step4-ui-agent**: Step 4 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every approved UX flow/screen (FR-derived and prerequisite alike), one at a time, and produces the visual design spec — components, layout, tokens, themes, cosmetics, brand compliance, and a concrete motion/animation implementation of the UX agent's transition model — grounded in deep, per-screen research (the full BR/FR/UX corpus, every project source document, live internet research, and named real mobile-app references) rather than invented from habit. Screen Priority (P0/P1/P2, set by Step 3) governs how much polish/motion investment a screen gets.… ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step4-ui-agent.md))
- **step5-test-scenarios-agent**: Step 5 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every FR (and its UX/UI), one at a time, and writes test scenarios covering functional and UI/interaction behavior, written before implementation exists. Tags each scenario by test-pyramid layer so the eventual automation isn't accidentally e2e-heavy. Invoke once 04-ui.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step5-test-scenarios-agent.md))
- **step6-impact-analysis-agent**: Step 6 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every FR, one at a time, and performs change impact analysis in two distinct parts: dependency identification, then risk assessment against those dependencies. This is the second (and last) point in the pipeline where a "worth check" — should this proceed at all, given what we now know — is asked. Invoke once 05-test-scenarios.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step6-impact-analysis-agent.md))
- **step7-tech-reqs-er-model-agent**: Step 7 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every impact-analyzed FR and produces technical requirements. Does NOT own the ER model — that is Step 7a's (step7a-er-model-agent) exclusive responsibility; Step 7 hands off to it once Sealed, and Step 7 itself does not seal until Step 8 begins, it seals on its own Tech-Reqs-only Definition of Done. Invoke once 06-impact-analysis.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step7-tech-reqs-er-model-agent.md))
- **step7a-er-model-agent**: Step 7a of the SDLC pipeline. Dedicated ER model design and database implementation agent. Produces a comprehensive, fully-traceable ER model through a mandatory three-pass internal process (draft, cross-validate, sign-off), then implements the actual database schema, migrations, and initialization scripts. This agent owns all database structural decisions and implementation. Invoke once 07-tech-reqs.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step7a-er-model-agent.md))
- **step8-security-performance-agent**: Step 8 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every technical requirement, one at a time, and applies STRIDE threat modeling plus performance threshold analysis before any implementation exists — this is the shift-left security gate, not a post-implementation audit. Invoke once 07-tech-reqs.md and 07a-er-model.md are both Sealed/Approved for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step8-security-performance-agent.md))
- **step9-implementation-agent**: Step 9 of the SDLC pipeline. Loops over every technical requirement, one at a time, and implements it — but only after writing a requirement-level comment block stating intent and reasoning for that requirement's code. No code is written until its comment is complete. Invoke once 08-security-performance.md is Sealed for a module. ([krishnakategaruai/forkhatri](https://github.com/krishnakategaruai/forkhatri/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Step9-implementation-agent.md))
- **step_1_entry**: 本文件是 Step 1 的新入口层，不替代 [step_1_topic.md](./step_1_topic.md)。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_1_entry.md) · ★35)
- **step_1_topic**: &gt; 旧版 Step 1 仅靠 5 轮问答让用户自述研究方向。v2.0 在对话中注入外部信息（文献量/趋势/gap/创新性），让用户做有依据的决策。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_1_topic.md) · ★35)
- **step_2_outline**: &gt; 基于 Step 1 的确定主题，生成论文大纲、关键词清单和章节证据需求表。含五维评审 + 导师视角检查 + 术语映射表生成。 &gt; **边界声明：** Step 1 负责问题结构收敛，但不压死研究发散；Step 2 是完整研究大纲 / 论文大纲的唯一生成层。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_2_outline.md) · ★35)
- **step_3_entry**: 本文件是 Step 3 的轻量路由层，不替代 [step_3_search_plan.md](./step_3_search_plan.md)。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_3_entry.md) · ★35)
- **step_3_search_plan**: &gt; 基于大纲和关键词，生成结构化检索方案。L1→L2→L3 分层路由架构 + 概念块布尔模型 + 反模式检查。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_3_search_plan.md) · ★35)
- **step_4_search_score**: &gt; 按 Step 3 方案的 L1→L2→L3 分层路由逐子课题检索，对结果进行 5 维度评分和 Tier 分级筛选。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_4_search_score.md) · ★35)
- **step_5_download**: &gt; ⚠️ **前置提醒：** &gt; 1. 进入真实下载前，如命中机构登录场景，agent 必须先提示用户在**同一个 CDP 浏览器**里完成机构登录，再继续执行下载。 &gt; 2. 如果宿主无法读取 `input()`，不得把下载脚本丢到后台硬跑；必须保留 `paper-temp/login_checkpoint.json`，并把该状态视为待用户确认登录，不是用户主动 skip。 &gt; 3. 用户完成机构登录后，恢复路径固定为 `python3 scripts/unified_download_router.py --resume-login-checkpoint paper-temp/login_checkpoint.json --output paper-temp/ --confirmed`。 &gt; 4. **未先问用户登录就直接启动真实下载是错误执行方式，不是 skill 缺陷。** &gt; &gt; ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_5_download.md) · ★35)
- **step_6_entry**: 本文件是 Step 6 的新入口层，不替代 [step_6_zotero.md](./step_6_zotero.md)。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_6_entry.md) · ★35)
- **step_6_zotero**: &gt; Step 6 负责把 Step 4 的 BibTeX 条目、Step 2 的论文大纲、Step 5/其他来源的 PDF 附件池整理成一个可审阅、可恢复、可追溯的 Zotero 文库整理计划，并尽可能完成低风险 Zotero 写入。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_6_zotero.md) · ★35)
- **step_7_entry**: 本文件是 Step 7 的路由层，不替代 [step_7_writing.md](./step_7_writing.md)。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_7_entry.md) · ★35)
- **step_7_writing**: &gt; Step 7 把 Zotero、PDF、BibTeX、证据包、已有草稿和图表资产转成可审计正文。它支持 direct-entry，但不能跳过证据边界、引用审计、公式门、图表授权和分层完成状态。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_7_writing.md) · ★35)
- **step_8_entry**: 本文件是 Step 8 的路由层，不替代 [step_8_polishing.md](./step_8_polishing.md)。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_8_entry.md) · ★35)
- **step_8_polishing**: &gt; 对既有成稿进行成稿级风险收口：全文一致性、术语终验、表达清理，以及**受约束补写 + 直接修改 + 修订后验证**。 &gt; Step 8 不负责主体写作，不补外部证据，不替代完整引用审计或修稿路线图；但它可以在现有正文、Step 7 三工件和评审/审计输入支撑下，对正文做修订性补写与局部增强。 ([bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow](https://github.com/bingyunjiang/more-paper-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/step_8_polishing.md) · ★35)
- **step_developer**: Executes a single declared step from PLAN.md — writes code and writes a handoff file. Called exclusively by the stepwise-builder skill for each step in Phase 2. Does NOT run the check — that is step_smoketest's job. ([HEchternacht/stepwise-builder](https://github.com/HEchternacht/stepwise-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/step_developer.md))
- **step_planner**: Decomposes any build request into a precise, layered, dependency-ordered plan saved to PLAN.md. Called exclusively by the stepwise-builder skill during Phase 1 and Phase 0b. ([HEchternacht/stepwise-builder](https://github.com/HEchternacht/stepwise-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/step_planner.md))
- **step_smoketest**: Runs the smoke check for a single completed step, updates PLAN.md, and returns PASS or FAIL. Called exclusively by the stepwise-builder skill after each step_developer run. ([HEchternacht/stepwise-builder](https://github.com/HEchternacht/stepwise-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/step_smoketest.md))
- **step_tester**: Runs end-to-end validation of the completed project. Called exclusively by the stepwise-builder skill after all steps are done. ([HEchternacht/stepwise-builder](https://github.com/HEchternacht/stepwise-builder/blob/HEAD/agents/step_tester.md))
- **stepback**: Only for the Cardinal supervise skill's fresh-context passes. Reads a brief and the code; changes nothing. ([cardinalhq/cardinal-agent-plugins](https://github.com/cardinalhq/cardinal-agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/agents/stepback.md) · 2 copies)
- **stepfunctions-scout**: Thin Step Functions ASL specialist. Spawn only when the RE plan lists orch-stepfunctions. ([SpillwaveSolutions/data-engineering-knowledge-capture](https://github.com/SpillwaveSolutions/data-engineering-knowledge-capture/blob/HEAD/agents/stepfunctions-scout.md))

- **stephen-king**: Review like Stephen King. Direct, no-BS feedback focused on story, character, and the honesty of the work. Hates adverbs. Loves story. ([howells/fiction](https://github.com/howells/fiction/blob/HEAD/agents/stephen-king.md) · ★24)
- **stephen-ml-engineer**: Role 10 · Stephen — A Stanford graduate who has operated large-scale ML at Google and Facebook — builds production ML that is reproducible and backed by evaluation, not demos. (wave: W5 (parallel with Phillip/Andrew)) ([taxideftis/bathos](https://github.com/taxideftis/bathos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stephen-ml-engineer.md) · ★31)
- **steps-architect-pro**: Heavy-reasoning architect for the steps protocol. Plans architectural phases and never writes code. Use this agent when a phase involves a DB migration, a protocol change, a cross-cutting refactor, or distributed logic and race-condition reasoning. Overkill for standard CRUD phases — routing this agent there is a defect. ([xilnick/project-constitution-protocol](https://github.com/xilnick/project-constitution-protocol/blob/HEAD/plugins/steps/harnesses/antigravity/agents/steps-architect-pro.md) · 3 copies)
- **steps-doubt**: Interim doubt-caster for a steps-run. Spawned twice (in parallel) by fwd:steps-run after every 4th approved step; each spawn gets exactly ONE probing question — "what are we least confident about?" or "what's the biggest thing being missed?". Thinks and writes caveman-style (ultra-terse, zero filler, zero hedging) but every claim carries an evidence pointer. Pulls all context itself (state, plan, diff, log) — nothing is pasted into its prompt. Read and inspect only; never modifies anything. ([baswenneker/fwd-skills](https://github.com/baswenneker/fwd-skills/blob/HEAD/obsolete/agents/steps-doubt.md))
- **steps-impl-reviewer**: Reviews a completed implementation in a clean context against the actual files on disk, checking correctness, conformance to plan, and gate integrity, and runs the gates itself rather than trusting the implementer's green. Use this agent after an implementer reports a phase complete and before anything is committed. ([xilnick/project-constitution-protocol](https://github.com/xilnick/project-constitution-protocol/blob/HEAD/plugins/steps/harnesses/antigravity/agents/steps-impl-reviewer.md) · 3 copies)
- **steps-implementer**: Executes an approved phase plan item by item, running each item's gate before moving on, and never weakening a gate to make it pass. Use this agent when PLAN.md is approved and code must be written, on a phase whose items each carry their own gate, or on work that must stop rather than silently loosen a check. - **steps-plan-reviewer**: Reviews a phase plan in a clean context and returns a verdict of approve, approve-with-amendments, or reject.
